1,012 entries, grok-rescue to gui-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ground-rules**: - **Install tools ONLY via devbox.** Missing a CLI? Add it to `devbox.json` (`devbox add &lt;pkg&gt;@&lt;ver&gt;`) then `devbox install` — reproducible, and exactly what CI runs. NEVER `curl`/download a binary: `.devbox/` is read-only and a hand-placed binary isn't on CI's PATH, so the gate goes red though it 'worked' for you. - **Prior-art before creating anything named** (doc, script, tracker entry) IN THIS ([teststuffstash/homelab](https://github.com/teststuffstash/homelab/blob/HEAD/agents/ground-rules.md))

- **grounding-checker**: Use this agent in the adversarial cross-check stage of a fan-out phase to verify that every extracted entry from ONE chunk traces to actual source text — and flag the ones that don't, so ungrounded (hallucinated or over-inferred) entries are filtered before they fold into canonical state. ([timothywarner-org/ai901-cert-buddy-claude](https://github.com/timothywarner-org/ai901-cert-buddy-claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/grounding-verifier.md) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **grounding-verifier**: Independently re-derives grounding findings — [CG#n] from one pinned repository, [DG#n] from one exported frame set — in two dispatches: derive mode, blind to each finding's answer (it refuses a dispatch that carries one), then compare mode, returning agree, extend, contradict or unprovable against its own blind result, which it never revises. A finding is not evidence until this agent has re-derived it. Does not check citations. Read-only. Uses Claude Opus. ([ihudak/ai-workflows](https://github.com/ihudak/ai-workflows/blob/HEAD/plugins/product-workflows/agents/grounding-verifier.md) · ★2)
- **grounding-verifier**: Independently re-derives a grounding finding from its own source — the pinned repository for a [CG#n] or a class-4 [DG#n], the exported frame set for a design-only [DG#n] — and returns agree / extend / contradict / unprovable with its own evidence. It does NOT check citations — checking a citation only proves the cited line exists. A finding is not evidence until this agent has re-derived it. Read-only. Uses Claude Opus. Use when asked to "run the guardrails", "check the harness", "run all the guardrail gates", or before a commit/PR touching numerics/physics/config/dispatch.
